   250	            }
   251	            serde_yaml_ng::Value::String(s) => ParamValue::String(s),
   252	            other => ParamValue::String(format!("{other:?}")),
   253	        });
   254	
   255	        Ok(ParamSpec {
   256	            param_type,
   257	            default,
   258	            min: raw.min,
   259	            max: raw.max,

   264	
   265	impl TryFrom<TemplateDefinitionRaw> for TemplateDefinition {
   266	    type Error = TemplateError;
   267	
   268	    fn try_from(raw: TemplateDefinitionRaw) -> Result<Self, Self::Error> {
   269	        let mut items = Vec::with_capacity(raw.layout.len());
   270	        for (idx, item) in raw.layout.into_iter().enumerate() {
   271	            let node = LayoutItem::try_from(item)
   272	                .map_err(|err| err.with_prefix(&format!("layout[{idx}]")))?;
   273	            items.push(node);
   274	        }
   275	
   276	        let mut params = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   277	        if let Some(raw_params) = raw.params {
   278	            for (key, spec_raw) in raw_params {
   279	                let spec = ParamSpec::try_from(spec_raw)
   280	                    .map_err(|err| err.with_prefix(&format!("params.{key}")))?;
   281	                params.insert(key, spec);
   282	            }
   283	        }
   284	        if let Some(raw_options) = raw.options {
   285	            for (key, values) in raw_options.0 {
   286	                params.entry(key).or_insert(ParamSpec {
   287	                    param_type: ParamType::Enum { values },
   288	                    default: None,
   289	                    min: None,
   290	                    max: None,
   291	                    description: None,
   292	                });
   293	            }
   294	        }
   295	
   296	        let format = TemplateFormat::try_from(raw.format)?;
   297	
   298	        Ok(TemplateDefinition {
   299	            id: raw.id,
   300	            name: raw.name,
   301	            description: raw.description.unwrap_or_default(),
   302	            group: raw.group.map(|g| g.trim().to_string()),
   303	            unit: raw.unit,
   304	            dpi: raw.dpi,
   305	            format,
   306	            params,
   307	            layout: Layout::Items(items),
   308	            version: raw.version,
   309	        })
   310	    }
   311	}

   133	    // Synchronous filesystem I/O. Acceptable for the single-user, local-templates-dir target and
   134	    // consistent with the synchronous Typst render path; revisit with spawn_blocking if it ever
   135	    // serves large dirs or remote storage.
   136	    fn reload(&self) -> Result<(usize, usize), TemplateRegistryError> {
   137	        let registry = TemplateRegistry::load_from_dir(&self.templates_dir)?;
   138	        let count = registry.len();
   139	        let broken_count = registry.broken().len();
   140	        if broken_count > 0 {
   141	            for b in registry.broken() {
   142	                tracing::warn!(filename = %b.filename, error = %b.error, "template failed to load");
   143	            }
   144	        }
   145	        self.templates.store(Arc::new(registry));
   146	        Ok((count, broken_count))
   147	    }
   148	}

   150	fn api_router() -> Router<Arc<AppState>> {
   151	    Router::new()
   152	        .route("/health", get(health))
   153	        .route("/templates", get(list_templates).post(create_template))
   154	        .route("/templates/reload", post(reload_templates))
   155	        .route(
   156	            "/templates/{id}",
   157	            get(get_template)
   158	                .put(replace_template)
   159	                .delete(delete_template),
   160	        )
   161	        .route("/templates/{id}/group", put(update_template_group))
   162	        .route("/templates/{id}/source", get(template_source))
   163	        .route("/templates/{id}/thumbnail", get(thumbnail))
   164	        .route("/printers", get(list_printers).post(create_printer))
   165	        .route("/printers/probe", post(probe_printer))
   166	        .route(
   167	            "/printers/{id}",
   168	            get(get_printer).put(replace_printer).delete(delete_printer),
   169	        )
   170	        .route(
   171	            "/printers/{id}/default",
   172	            post(set_printer_default).delete(clear_printer_default),
   173	        )
   174	        .route(
   175	            "/connections",
   176	            get(list_connections).post(create_connection),
   177	        )
   178	        .route(
   179	            "/connections/{id}",
   180	            get(get_connection_h)
   181	                .put(update_connection_h)
   182	                .delete(delete_connection_h),
   183	        )
   184	        .route("/connections/{id}/schema", get(connection_schema))
   185	        .route("/connections/{id}/browse", post(connection_browse))
   186	        .route(
   187	            "/connections/{id}/materialize",
   188	            post(connection_materialize),
   189	        )
   190	        .route("/variables", get(get_variables))
   191	        .route("/variables/{key}", put(put_variable))
   192	        .route("/settings", get(get_settings))
   193	        .route("/settings/{key}", put(put_setting).delete(delete_setting))
   194	        .route("/datetime-formats/preview", post(preview_datetime_format))
   195	        .route("/render/label", post(render_label))
   196	        .route("/batch", post(batch))
   197	        .route(
   198	            "/print",
   199	            post(print_label).layer(DefaultBodyLimit::max(64 * 1024)),
   200	        )
   201	        .route("/import/csv", post(import_csv))
   202	        .route("/favorites", get(list_favorites))
   203	        .route(
   204	            "/favorites/{template_id}",
   205	            put(add_favorite).delete(remove_favorite),

   207	        .route("/recent-templates", get(recent_templates))
   208	        .route("/auth/setup", post(setup))

   210	        .route("/auth/logout", post(logout))
   211	        .route("/auth/me", get(me))
   212	        .route("/auth/password", post(change_password))
   213	        .route("/users", get(list_users).post(create_user_h))
   214	        .route("/users/{id}", axum::routing::delete(delete_user_h))
   215	        .route("/tokens", get(list_tokens).post(create_token_h))
   216	        .route("/tokens/{id}", axum::routing::delete(delete_token_h))
   217	        // Serve the OpenAPI doc from an explicit route so it resolves at /api/openapi.json under the
   218	        // `/api` nest (SwaggerUi's own `.url()` serving route gets double-prefixed when nested).
   219	        .route("/openapi.json", get(openapi_json))
   220	        // SwaggerUi serves the UI at /api/docs/ (trailing slash).
   221	        .merge(SwaggerUi::new("/docs").url("/api/openapi.json", ApiDoc::openapi()))
   222	}

   228	pub fn app(state: Arc<AppState>) -> Router {
   229	    let assets = tower_http::services::ServeDir::new(state.ui_dir().join("assets"));
   230	    let api = api_router().layer(axum::middleware::from_fn_with_state(
   231	        state.clone(),
   232	        crate::middleware::require_auth,
   233	    ));
   234	    Router::new()
   235	        .nest("/api", api)
   236	        .nest_service("/assets", assets)
   237	        .fallback(fallback)
   238	        .layer(TraceLayer::new_for_http())
   239	        .with_state(state)
   240	}

   242	async fn fallback(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, uri: axum::http::Uri) -> Response {
   243	    if uri.path() == "/api" || uri.path().starts_with("/api/") {
   244	        return AppError::not_found(uri.path()).into_response();
   245	    }
   246	    // SPA: serve index.html for any non-API, non-asset route (client-side routing).
   247	    match tokio::fs::read(state.ui_dir().join("index.html")).await {
   248	        Ok(bytes) => (
   249	            axum::http::StatusCode::OK,
   250	            [("content-type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")],
   251	            bytes,
   252	        )
   253	            .into_response(),
   254	        Err(_) => (
   255	            axum::http::StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
   256	            "UI not built; run `npm --prefix ui run build`",
   257	        )
   258	            .into_response(),
   259	    }
   260	}

   263	    get,

   266	        (status = 200, description = "Service is healthy", body = HealthResponse)
   267	    )
   268	)]
   269	pub async fn health() -> impl IntoResponse {

   272	    })

   275	#[derive(Debug, Default, serde::Deserialize)]
   276	pub struct TemplateListQuery {
   277	    pub group: Option<String>,
   278	}
   279	
   280	#[utoipa::path(
   281	    get,
   282	    path = "/templates",
   283	    params(
   284	        ("group" = Option<String>, Query, description = "Filter templates by group. Omit for all templates; pass empty (?group=) for ungrouped templates.")
   285	    ),
   286	    responses(
   287	        (status = 200, description = "List templates", body = TemplateList)
   288	    )
   289	)]
   290	pub async fn list_templates(
   291	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
   292	    Query(query): Query<TemplateListQuery>,
   293	) -> impl IntoResponse {
   294	    let registry = state.templates.load_full();
   295	    let mut templates = registry.summaries();
   296	    if let Some(ref group) = query.group {
   297	        let stripped = group.trim();
   298	        if stripped.is_empty() {
   299	            templates.retain(|t| t.group.is_none());
   300	        } else {
   301	            templates.retain(|t| t.group.as_deref() == Some(stripped));
   302	        }
   303	    }
   304	    let broken = registry
   305	        .broken()
   306	        .iter()
   307	        .map(|b| crate::models::BrokenTemplateSummary {
   308	            filename: b.filename.clone(),
   309	            error: b.error.clone(),
   310	        })
   311	        .collect();
   312	    Json(TemplateList { templates, broken })
   313	}
   314	
   315	#[utoipa::path(
   316	    post,
   317	    path = "/templates/reload",
   318	    responses(
   319	        (status = 200, description = "Templates reloaded from disk", body = ReloadResponse),
   320	        (status = 500, description = "Failed to read the templates directory", body = ErrorResponse)
   321	    )
   322	)]
   323	pub async fn reload_templates(
   324	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
   325	) -> Result<Json<ReloadResponse>, AppError> {
   326	    let (count, broken_count) = state.reload()?;
   327	    Ok(Json(ReloadResponse {
   328	        count,
   329	        broken_count,
   330	    }))
   331	}
   332	
   333	fn template_file_path(dir: &std::path::Path, id: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, AppError> {
   334	    if id.is_empty()
   335	        || !id
   336	            .chars()
   337	            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '-' || c == '_')
   338	    {
   339	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
   340	            Reason::TemplateIdInvalid,
   341	            format!(
   342	                "template id '{id}' must be non-empty and contain only letters, digits, '-' or '_'"
   343	            ),
   344	        ));
   345	    }
   346	    Ok(dir.join(format!("{id}.yaml")))
   347	}
   348	
   349	/// The on-disk file backing `id`, per the registry. `None` for an id the registry does not hold.
   350	///
   351	/// It resolves through the registry and never guesses at a filename: `y1.yml` may well declare
   352	/// `id: other`, so treating a matching filename as the backing file would let `DELETE /templates/y1`
   353	/// unlink another template's file. A stray file the registry never loaded stays a filesystem
   354	/// concern (#140).
   355	fn existing_template_file(
   356	    registry: &TemplateRegistry,
   357	    dir: &std::path::Path,
   358	    id: &str,
   359	) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>, AppError> {
   360	    // Called for its id validation; the conventional path it returns is not the answer here.
   361	    template_file_path(dir, id)?;
   362	    Ok(registry.path(id).map(std::path::Path::to_path_buf))
   363	}
   364	
   365	fn parse_and_validate(body: &str) -> Result<TemplateDefinition, AppError> {
   366	    let template = parse_template(body)
   367	        .map_err(|err| AppError::template_invalid(Reason::TemplateParseFailed, err.to_string()))?;
   368	    template
   369	        .validate()
   370	        .map_err(|err| AppError::template_invalid(Reason::TemplateValidationFailed, err))?;
   371	    Ok(template)
   372	}
   373	
   374	fn write_template_file(path: &std::path::Path, body: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
   375	    let dir = path.parent().ok_or_else(|| {
   376	        AppError::render_failed(Reason::TemplatePathInvalid, "invalid template path")
   377	    })?;
   378	    let file_name = path
   379	        .file_name()
   380	        .and_then(|name| name.to_str())
   381	        .ok_or_else(|| {
   382	            AppError::render_failed(Reason::TemplatePathInvalid, "invalid template path")
   383	        })?;
   384	    let nonce = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   385	        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   386	        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
   387	        .unwrap_or(0);
   388	    let tmp = dir.join(format!(".{file_name}.{nonce}.tmp"));
   389	    std::fs::write(&tmp, body).map_err(|err| {
   390	        AppError::render_failed(
   391	            Reason::TemplateWriteFailed,
   392	            format!("failed to write template: {err}"),
   393	        )
   394	    })?;
   395	    std::fs::rename(&tmp, path).map_err(|err| {
   396	        AppError::render_failed(
   397	            Reason::TemplateWriteFailed,
   398	            format!("failed to persist template: {err}"),
   399	        )
   400	    })?;
   401	    Ok(())
   402	}
   403	
   404	#[utoipa::path(
   405	    post,
   406	    path = "/templates",
   407	    request_body(content = String, description = "Template YAML", content_type = "text/yaml"),
   408	    responses(
   409	        (status = 201, description = "Template created", body = TemplateDetail),
   410	        (status = 409, description = "Template id already exists", body = ErrorResponse),
   411	        (status = 422, description = "Invalid template", body = ErrorResponse)
   412	    )
   413	)]
   414	pub async fn create_template(
   415	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
   416	    body: String,
   417	) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
   418	    let template = parse_and_validate(&body)?;
   419	    let id = template.id.clone();
   420	    let path = template_file_path(&state.templates_dir, &id)?;
   421	    let _guard = state.write_lock.lock().await;
   422	    // Registry first: the id may already be held by a file under a different name, which
   423	    // `path.exists()` misses. The path check still covers a file the registry has not loaded. Since
   424	    // #181 the reload below no longer fails on a duplicate id, so this guard is the only thing
   425	    // standing between a write and a silent collision, and it misses a file added to the directory
   426	    // since the last reload (#184).
   427	    if state.templates.load_full().get(&id).is_some() || path.exists() {
   428	        return Err(AppError::template_exists(&id));
   429	    }
   430	    write_template_file(&path, &body)?;
   431	    state.reload()?;
   432	    let detail = state.templates.load_full().detail(&id).ok_or_else(|| {
   433	        AppError::render_failed(
   434	            Reason::TemplateMissingAfterWrite,
   435	            "template missing after write",
   436	        )
   437	    })?;
   438	    Ok((axum::http::StatusCode::CREATED, Json(detail)).into_response())
   439	}
   440	
   441	#[utoipa::path(
   442	    put,
   443	    path = "/templates/{id}",
   444	    params(("id" = String, Path, description = "Template ID")),
   445	    request_body(content = String, description = "Template YAML", content_type = "text/yaml"),
   446	    responses(
   447	        (status = 200, description = "Template replaced", body = TemplateDetail),
   448	        (status = 400, description = "Body id does not match path id", body = ErrorResponse),
   449	        (status = 404, description = "Template not found", body = ErrorResponse),
   450	        (status = 422, description = "Invalid template", body = ErrorResponse)
   451	    )
   452	)]
   453	pub async fn replace_template(
   454	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
   455	    Path(id): Path<String>,
   456	    body: String,
   457	) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
   458	    let template = parse_and_validate(&body)?;
   459	    if template.id != id {
   460	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
   461	            Reason::TemplateIdMismatch,
   462	            format!(
   463	                "template id in body ('{}') must match path id ('{id}')",
   464	                template.id
   465	            ),
   466	        ));
   467	    }
   468	    // Resolved under the lock, not before it: an in-flight delete would otherwise unlink the file
   469	    // between the resolve and the write, and this handler would recreate the template it removed.
   470	    let _guard = state.write_lock.lock().await;
   471	    let path = existing_template_file(&state.templates.load_full(), &state.templates_dir, &id)?
   472	        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::template_not_found(id.clone()))?;
   473	    write_template_file(&path, &body)?;
   474	    state.reload()?;
   475	    let detail = state.templates.load_full().detail(&id).ok_or_else(|| {
   476	        AppError::render_failed(
   477	            Reason::TemplateMissingAfterWrite,
   478	            "template missing after write",
   479	        )
   480	    })?;
   481	    Ok((axum::http::StatusCode::OK, Json(detail)).into_response())
   482	}
   483	
   484	#[utoipa::path(
   485	    put,
   486	    path = "/templates/{id}/group",
   487	    params(("id" = String, Path, description = "Template ID")),
   488	    request_body(content = TemplateGroupUpdate, description = "Group assignment"),
   489	    responses(
   490	        (status = 200, description = "Template group updated", body = TemplateDetail),
   491	        (status = 400, description = "Invalid id or request body", body = ErrorResponse),
   492	        (status = 404, description = "Template not found", body = ErrorResponse),
   493	        (status = 422, description = "Invalid group name or unpatchable template", body = ErrorResponse),
   494	        (status = 500, description = "File write or registry reload failed", body = ErrorResponse)
   495	    )
   496	)]
   497	pub async fn update_template_group(
   498	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
   499	    Path(id): Path<String>,
   500	    payload: Result<Json<TemplateGroupUpdate>, JsonRejection>,
   501	) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
   502	    let Json(update) = payload.map_err(AppError::from)?;
   503	    let _guard = state.write_lock.lock().await;
   504	    let path = existing_template_file(&state.templates.load_full(), &state.templates_dir, &id)?
   505	        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::template_not_found(id.clone()))?;
   506	    let yaml = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)
   507	        .map_err(|err| AppError::render_failed(Reason::TemplateRegistryIo, err.to_string()))?;
   508	    let patched = patch_template_group(&yaml, update.group.as_deref())?;
   509	    if patched != yaml {
   510	        write_template_file(&path, &patched)?;
   511	        state.reload()?;
   512	    }
   513	    let detail = state.templates.load_full().detail(&id).ok_or_else(|| {
   514	        AppError::render_failed(
   515	            Reason::TemplateMissingAfterWrite,
   516	            "template missing after write",
   517	        )
   518	    })?;
   519	    Ok((axum::http::StatusCode::OK, Json(detail)).into_response())
   520	}

    40	#[derive(Debug)]
    41	pub struct AppError {
    42	    status: StatusCode,
    43	    code: &'static str,
    44	    message: String,
    45	    details: Option<Value>,
    46	    reason: Option<Reason>,
    47	}
    48	
    49	/// One label's validation failure within a batch (its 0-based index + the error code/reason/message).
    50	#[derive(Debug, serde::Serialize)]
    51	pub struct BatchFailure {
    52	    pub index: usize,
    53	    pub code: &'static str,
    54	    /// Present exactly when the failure's code carries a reason (SPEC §10.1). A per-label failure
    55	    /// can be a code outside the migrated four, so this is optional rather than required.
    56	    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    57	    pub reason: Option<&'static str>,
    58	    pub message: String,
    59	}
    60	
    61	impl AppError {
    62	    fn new(
    63	        status: StatusCode,
    64	        code: &'static str,
    65	        message: impl Into<String>,
    66	        details: Option<Value>,
    67	    ) -> Self {
    68	        Self {
    69	            status,
    70	            code,
    71	            message: message.into(),
    72	            details,
    73	            reason: None,
    74	        }
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    /// Build an error that carries a reason (ADR-0052). `extra` is merged alongside `reason` in
    78	    /// `details`; taking a `Map` rather than a `Value` makes a non-object `details` unrepresentable,
    79	    /// so the merge cannot lose data. This is the only writer of both `details` and `reason` for a
    80	    /// reasoned error, so the two cannot diverge.
    81	    fn reasoned(
    82	        status: StatusCode,
    83	        code: &'static str,
    84	        reason: Reason,
    85	        message: impl Into<String>,
    86	        extra: Option<serde_json::Map<String, Value>>,
    87	    ) -> Self {
    88	        let mut details = extra.unwrap_or_default();
    89	        details.insert("reason".to_string(), Value::from(reason.as_slug()));
    90	        Self {
    91	            status,
    92	            code,
    93	            message: message.into(),
    94	            details: Some(Value::Object(details)),
    95	            reason: Some(reason),
    96	        }
    97	    }
    98	
    99	    /// The `details.reason` slug, when this error carries one (SPEC §10.1).
   100	    pub fn reason(&self) -> Option<&'static str> {
   101	        self.reason.map(Reason::as_slug)
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    pub fn message_text(&self) -> String {
   105	        self.message.clone()
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    /// A JSON body that axum could not parse. Keeps the parser's own text under `details.error`.
   109	    pub fn malformed_json(parser_error: String) -> Self {
   110	        let mut extra = serde_json::Map::new();
   111	        extra.insert("error".to_string(), Value::from(parser_error));
   112	        Self::reasoned(
   113	            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
   114	            CODE_INVALID_REQUEST,
   115	            Reason::JsonMalformed,
   116	            "Malformed JSON body",
   117	            Some(extra),
   118	        )
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    pub fn batch_invalid(failures: Vec<BatchFailure>) -> Self {
   122	        Self::new(
   123	            StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
   124	            CODE_BATCH_INVALID,
   125	            "one or more labels in the batch are invalid",
   126	            Some(json!({ "failures": failures })),
   127	        )
   128	    }

   801	
   802	fn validate_param_name(name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
   803	    if name.is_empty() {
   804	        return Err("parameter name must not be empty".to_string());
   805	    }
   806	    if name == "datetime" || name == "vars" {
   807	        return Err(format!("parameter name '{name}' is reserved"));
   808	    }
   809	    if name.starts_with("datetime.") || name.starts_with("vars.") {
   810	        return Err(format!("parameter name '{name}' uses a reserved prefix"));
   811	    }
   812	    if !name
   813	        .chars()
   814	        .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-')
   815	    {
   816	        return Err(format!(
   817	            "parameter name '{name}' contains invalid characters; must match ^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$"
   818	        ));
   819	    }
   820	    Ok(())
   821	}
   822	
   823	fn validate_param_spec(name: &str, spec: &ParamSpec) -> Result<(), String> {
   824	    match &spec.param_type {
   825	        ParamType::Enum { values } => {
   826	            if values.is_empty() {
   827	                return Err(format!("parameter '{name}' enum values must not be empty"));
   828	            }
   829	            if values.iter().any(|opt| opt.trim().is_empty()) {
   830	                return Err("options must not contain empty values".to_string());
   831	            }
   832	            if let Some(default) = &spec.default {
   833	                let default_str = match default {
   834	                    crate::models::ParamValue::String(s) => s.as_str(),
   835	                    crate::models::ParamValue::Integer(i) => {
   836	                        let s = i.to_string();
   837	                        if !values.contains(&s) {
   838	                            return Err(format!(
   839	                                "parameter '{name}' default '{i}' is not in enum values"
   840	                            ));
   841	                        }
   842	                        return Ok(());
   843	                    }
   844	                    _ => "",
   845	                };
   846	                if !values.iter().any(|v| v == default_str) {
   847	                    return Err(format!(
   848	                        "parameter '{name}' default '{default_str}' is not in enum values"
   849	                    ));
   850	                }
   851	            }
   852	        }
   853	        ParamType::Length | ParamType::Number | ParamType::Integer => {
   854	            if let (Some(min), Some(max)) = (spec.min, spec.max) {
   855	                if min > max {
   856	                    return Err(format!(
   857	                        "parameter '{name}' min ({min}) must be <= max ({max})"
   858	                    ));
   859	                }
   860	            }
   861	        }
   862	        _ => {}
   863	    }
   864	    Ok(())
   865	}
   866	
   867	fn check_param_ref(
   868	    params: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, ParamSpec>,
   869	    name: &str,
   870	    context: &str,
   871	    allowed_types: &[&str],
   872	) -> Result<(), String> {
   873	    let spec = params
   874	        .get(name)
   875	        .ok_or_else(|| format!("undeclared parameter '{name}' referenced in {context}"))?;
   876	    let matches_type = match &spec.param_type {
   877	        ParamType::Length => allowed_types.contains(&"length"),
   878	        ParamType::Number => allowed_types.contains(&"number"),
   879	        ParamType::Integer => allowed_types.contains(&"integer"),
   880	        ParamType::String { .. } => allowed_types.contains(&"string"),
   881	        ParamType::Boolean => allowed_types.contains(&"boolean"),
   882	        ParamType::Enum { .. } => allowed_types.contains(&"enum"),
   883	    };
   884	    if !matches_type {
   885	        return Err(format!(
   886	            "parameter '{name}' of type {:?} cannot be used in {context}",
   887	            spec.param_type
   888	        ));
   889	    }
   890	    Ok(())
   891	}
   892	
   893	fn validate_when_references(
   894	    when: Option<&std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>>,
   895	    params: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, ParamSpec>,
   896	) -> Result<(), String> {
   897	    if let Some(when) = when {
   898	        for (name, val) in when {
   899	            let spec = params.get(name).ok_or_else(|| {
   900	                format!("undeclared parameter '{name}' referenced in when condition")
   901	            })?;
   902	            if let ParamType::Enum { values } = &spec.param_type {
   903	                if !values.iter().any(|v| v == val) {
   904	                    return Err(format!(
   905	                        "when condition for '{name}' references '{val}' which is not in enum values"
   906	                    ));
   907	                }
   908	            }
   909	        }
   910	    }
   911	    Ok(())
   912	}

   383	
   384	impl From<JsonRejection> for AppError {
   385	    fn from(rejection: JsonRejection) -> Self {
   386	        if rejection.status() == StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE {
   387	            return AppError::payload_too_large("Request body too large");
   388	        }
   389	        let message = rejection.body_text();
   390	        tracing::warn!(message = %message, "json request rejected");
   391	        match rejection {
   392	            JsonRejection::MissingJsonContentType(_) => {
   393	                AppError::unsupported_media_type("Content-Type must be application/json")
   394	            }
   395	            JsonRejection::JsonSyntaxError(_) | JsonRejection::JsonDataError(_) => {
   396	                AppError::malformed_json(message)
   397	            }
   398	            JsonRejection::BytesRejection(_) => {
   399	                AppError::invalid_request(Reason::RequestBodyInvalid, "Invalid request body")
   400	            }
   401	            _ => AppError::invalid_request(Reason::RequestBodyInvalid, "Invalid JSON request"),
   402	        }
   403	    }
   404	}
   405	
   406	impl From<PathRejection> for AppError {
   407	    fn from(_rejection: PathRejection) -> Self {
   408	        AppError::invalid_request(Reason::PathParamInvalid, "Invalid path parameter")
   409	    }
   410	}
   411	
   412	impl From<TemplateRegistryError> for AppError {
   413	    fn from(err: TemplateRegistryError) -> Self {
   414	        let message = err.to_string();
   415	        match err {
   416	            TemplateRegistryError::Io { .. } => {
   417	                AppError::render_failed(Reason::TemplateRegistryIo, message)
   418	            }
   419	            TemplateRegistryError::Parse { .. } => {
   420	                AppError::template_invalid(Reason::TemplateParseFailed, message)
   421	            }
   422	            TemplateRegistryError::Validation { .. } => {
   423	                AppError::template_invalid(Reason::TemplateValidationFailed, message)
   424	            }
   425	            TemplateRegistryError::DuplicateId { .. } => {
   426	                AppError::template_invalid(Reason::TemplateDuplicateId, message)
   427	            }
   428	        }
   429	    }
   430	}
   431	
   432	impl From<crate::connector::ConnectorError> for AppError {
   433	    fn from(err: crate::connector::ConnectorError) -> Self {
   434	        use crate::connector::ConnectorError::*;
   435	        // These codes sit outside the four that carry a `details.reason` (ADR-0052): they describe
   436	        // upstream transport, not a layout or request fault.
   437	        let (status, code, message): (StatusCode, &'static str, String) = match err {
   438	            AuthFailed => (
   439	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   440	                "ConnectorAuthFailed",
   441	                "upstream authentication failed".into(),
   442	            ),
   443	            Forbidden => (
   444	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   445	                "ConnectorForbidden",
   446	                "upstream forbidden".into(),
   447	            ),
   448	            ConnectionFailed(m) => (StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY, "ConnectorUnreachable", m),
   449	            InvalidFilter(m) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "InvalidFilter", m),
   450	            UpstreamSchemaMismatch(m) => (StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY, "UpstreamSchemaMismatch", m),
   451	            RateLimited => (
   452	                StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
   453	                "RateLimited",
   454	                "upstream rate limited".into(),
   455	            ),
   456	            BudgetExceeded => (
   457	                StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
   458	                "BudgetExceeded",
   459	                "too many rows requested".into(),
   460	            ),
   461	            Upstream(m) => (StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY, "Upstream", m),
   462	        };
   463	        Self::new(status, code, message, None)
   464	    }
   465	}

   473	#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
   474	pub enum TemplateError {
   475	    Yaml { path: String, msg: String },
   476	    Validation { path: String, msg: String },
   477	}
   478	
   479	impl TemplateError {
   480	    pub fn with_prefix(self, prefix: &str) -> Self {
   481	        match self {
   482	            TemplateError::Yaml { path, msg } => TemplateError::Yaml {
   483	                path: join_path(prefix, &path),
   484	                msg,
   485	            },
   486	            TemplateError::Validation { path, msg } => TemplateError::Validation {
   487	                path: join_path(prefix, &path),
   488	                msg,
   489	            },
   490	        }
   491	    }
   492	
   493	    pub fn at(self, segment: &str) -> Self {
   494	        match self {
   495	            TemplateError::Yaml { path, msg } => TemplateError::Yaml {
   496	                path: join_path(&path, segment),
   497	                msg,
   498	            },
   499	            TemplateError::Validation { path, msg } => TemplateError::Validation {
   500	                path: join_path(&path, segment),
   501	                msg,
   502	            },
   503	        }
   504	    }
   505	}
   506	
   507	fn join_path(prefix: &str, suffix: &str) -> String {
   508	    if prefix.is_empty() {
   509	        return suffix.to_string();
   510	    }
   511	    if suffix.is_empty() {
   512	        return prefix.to_string();
   513	    }
   514	    if suffix.starts_with('[') {
   515	        format!("{prefix}{suffix}")
   516	    } else {
   517	        format!("{prefix}.{suffix}")
   518	    }
   519	}
   520	
   521	impl std::fmt::Display for TemplateError {
   522	    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   523	        match self {
   524	            TemplateError::Yaml { path, msg } => {
   525	                if path.is_empty() {
   526	                    write!(f, "yaml error: {msg}")
   527	                } else {
   528	                    write!(f, "yaml error at {path}: {msg}")
   529	                }
   530	            }
   531	            TemplateError::Validation { path, msg } => {
   532	                if path.is_empty() {
   533	                    write!(f, "validation error: {msg}")
   534	                } else {
   535	                    write!(f, "validation error at {path}: {msg}")
   536	                }
   537	            }
   538	        }
   539	    }
   540	}
   541	
   542	impl std::error::Error for TemplateError {}

   544	#[cfg(test)]
   545	mod tests {
   546	    use super::{AppError, BatchFailure};
   547	    use crate::reason::Reason;
   548	    use axum::http::StatusCode;
   549	
   550	    #[test]
   551	    fn container_padding_no_room_reason_is_registered() {
   552	        assert_eq!(
   553	            Reason::ContainerPaddingNoRoom.as_slug(),
   554	            "container_padding_no_room"
   555	        );
   556	    }
   557	
   558	    /// The SPEC §10.1 table is the published contract; the enum is what the code emits. If they
   559	    /// drift, clients switch on slugs that either no longer exist or were never documented. Checked
   560	    /// in both directions deliberately: an undocumented slug is as bad as a phantom one.
   561	    #[test]
   562	    fn spec_documents_every_reason_and_invents_none() {
   563	        use crate::reason::Reason;
   564	        use std::collections::HashSet;
   565	
   566	        let spec = std::fs::read_to_string(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/docs/SPEC.md"))
   567	            .expect("read SPEC.md");
   568	        let section = spec
   569	            .split("### 10.1")
   570	            .nth(1)
   571	            .expect("SPEC.md must have a §10.1 reason section");
   572	        let section = section.split("\n## ").next().unwrap_or(section);
   573	
   574	        // Column 2 is the slug, in backticks. The header cell reads "Reason" without backticks and
   575	        // the separator row has none either, so both drop out here.
   576	        let mut documented: HashSet<String> = section
   577	            .lines()
   578	            .filter(|line| line.starts_with('|'))
   579	            .filter_map(|line| line.split('|').nth(2))
   580	            .map(str::trim)
   581	            .filter_map(|cell| cell.strip_prefix('`')?.strip_suffix('`'))
   582	            .map(str::to_string)
   583	            .collect();
   584	
   585	        let declared: HashSet<&str> = Reason::ALL.iter().map(|r| r.as_slug()).collect();
   586	
   587	        // Also collect reasons documented in openspec specs/changes
   588	        let manifest_dir = env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR");
   589	        let openspec_dir = std::path::Path::new(manifest_dir).join("openspec");
   590	        if openspec_dir.is_dir() {
   591	            fn scan_dir(
   592	                dir: &std::path::Path,
   593	                declared: &HashSet<&str>,
   594	                documented: &mut HashSet<String>,
   595	            ) {
   596	                if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) {
   597	                    for entry in entries.flatten() {
   598	                        let path = entry.path();
   599	                        if path.is_dir() {
   600	                            scan_dir(&path, declared, documented);
   601	                        } else if path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("md") {
   602	                            if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
   603	                                for slug in declared {
   604	                                    if content.contains(&format!("`{slug}`")) {
   605	                                        documented.insert(slug.to_string());
   606	                                    }
   607	                                }
   608	                            }
   609	                        }
   610	                    }
   611	                }
   612	            }
   613	            scan_dir(&openspec_dir, &declared, &mut documented);
   614	        }
   615	
   616	        let documented_refs: HashSet<&str> = documented.iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
   617	
   618	        let mut undocumented: Vec<_> = declared.difference(&documented_refs).collect();
   619	        undocumented.sort_unstable();
   620	        assert!(
   621	            undocumented.is_empty(),
   622	            "reasons missing from specs: {undocumented:?}"
   623	        );
   624	
   625	        let mut phantom: Vec<_> = documented_refs.difference(&declared).collect();
   626	        phantom.sort_unstable();
   627	        assert!(
   628	            phantom.is_empty(),
   629	            "specs document reasons that do not exist: {phantom:?}"
   630	        );
   631	    }
   632	
   633	    /// Decision 4 of ADR-0052: a per-label failure carries `reason` exactly when its code is one of
   634	    /// the migrated four. Both halves matter — a required field would contradict the scoping, and a
   635	    /// missing one would leave the nested failures prose-discriminated.
   636	    #[test]
   637	    fn batch_failure_carries_reason_only_for_reasoned_codes() {
   638	        use crate::reason::Reason;
   639	
   640	        let reasoned = AppError::template_invalid(Reason::TemplateParseFailed, "boom");
   641	        let failure = BatchFailure {
   642	            index: 0,
   643	            code: reasoned.code(),
   644	            reason: reasoned.reason(),
   645	            message: reasoned.message_text(),
   646	        };
   647	        let json = serde_json::to_value(&failure).expect("serialize");
   648	        assert_eq!(json["reason"], "template_parse_failed");
   649	
   650	        let unreasoned = AppError::missing_field("code");
   651	        let failure = BatchFailure {
   652	            index: 1,
   653	            code: unreasoned.code(),
   654	            reason: unreasoned.reason(),
   655	            message: unreasoned.message_text(),
   656	        };
   657	        let json = serde_json::to_value(&failure).expect("serialize");
   658	        assert!(
   659	            json.get("reason").is_none(),
   660	            "an unreasoned code must omit the key, got {json}"
   661	        );
   662	    }

   664	    #[test]
   665	    fn connector_errors_keep_their_codes_and_statuses() {
   666	        use crate::connector::ConnectorError;
   667	        let cases = [
   668	            (
   669	                ConnectorError::AuthFailed,
   670	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   671	                "ConnectorAuthFailed",
   672	            ),
   673	            (
   674	                ConnectorError::Forbidden,
   675	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   676	                "ConnectorForbidden",
   677	            ),
   678	            (
   679	                ConnectorError::RateLimited,
   680	                StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
   681	                "RateLimited",
   682	            ),
   683	            (
   684	                ConnectorError::BudgetExceeded,
   685	                StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
   686	                "BudgetExceeded",
   687	            ),
   688	            (
   689	                ConnectorError::InvalidFilter("x".into()),
   690	                StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
   691	                "InvalidFilter",
   692	            ),
   693	            (
   694	                ConnectorError::ConnectionFailed("x".into()),
   695	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   696	                "ConnectorUnreachable",
   697	            ),
   698	            (
   699	                ConnectorError::UpstreamSchemaMismatch("x".into()),
   700	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   701	                "UpstreamSchemaMismatch",
   702	            ),
   703	            (
   704	                ConnectorError::Upstream("x".into()),
   705	                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
   706	                "Upstream",
   707	            ),
   708	        ];
   709	        for (err, status, code) in cases {
   710	            let app_err = AppError::from(err);
   711	            // `status` is private, but this module is a child of `errors`, so it is visible here.
   712	            assert_eq!(app_err.status, status, "status for {code}");
   713	            assert_eq!(app_err.code(), code);
   714	        }
   715	    }

     1	//! The `details.reason` vocabulary (ADR-0052).
     2	//!
     3	//! Each slug is API: clients switch on it, so renaming one is a breaking change. The macro keeps
     4	//! `ALL` structurally complete — a variant cannot be added without appearing in it, which is what
     5	//! makes the completeness test in `errors.rs` meaningful. Slugs are written out beside their
     6	//! variants rather than derived from them, so renaming a variant does not silently move the wire
     7	//! value.
     8	
     9	macro_rules! reasons {
    10	    ($($variant:ident => $slug:literal,)+) => {
    11	        /// A stable, machine-readable cause, serialized as `details.reason`.
    12	        #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    13	        pub enum Reason {
    14	            $($variant,)+
    15	        }
    16	
    17	        impl Reason {
    18	            /// Every reason, in declaration order.
    19	            pub const ALL: &'static [Reason] = &[$(Reason::$variant,)+];
    20	
    21	            /// The wire slug. Part of the API contract; see SPEC §10.1.
    22	            pub fn as_slug(self) -> &'static str {
    23	                match self {
    24	                    $(Reason::$variant => $slug,)+
    25	                }
    26	            }
    27	        }
    28	    };
    29	}

codex
I’ve found one contract-level problem already: the raw model uses `Option<String>` for a present `group: null`/empty YAML value, which collapses it into “absent” before validation can reject it. I’m checking whether that same absent/null collapse exists on the move endpoint request body and whether tests cover or mask it.

  1115	pub async fn probe_printer(Json(req): Json<ProbeRequest>) -> Result<Json<ProbeResponse>, AppError> {
  1116	    let kind = req.kind.as_deref().unwrap_or("cups").to_string();
  1117	    let config = req.config;
  1118	    crate::driver::validate_config(&kind, &config)
  1119	        .map_err(|e| AppError::printer_invalid(e.to_string()))?;
  1120	    let driver = crate::driver::build_driver(&kind, &config)
  1121	        .map_err(|e| AppError::printer_invalid(e.to_string()))?;
  1122	    Ok(Json(match driver.probe().await {
  1123	        crate::driver::ProbeOutcome::Ok(c) => ProbeResponse::ok(c),
  1124	        crate::driver::ProbeOutcome::Unreachable(d) => ProbeResponse::Unreachable { detail: d },
  1125	    }))
  1126	}

  1167	where
  1168	    D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,
  1169	{
  1170	    serde::Deserialize::deserialize(deserializer).map(Some)

  1177	pub(crate) fn validate_and_normalize_url(raw: &str, field_name: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
  1178	    let trimmed = raw.trim();
  1179	    let parsed = url::Url::parse(trimmed).map_err(|_| {
  1180	        AppError::invalid_request(Reason::BaseUrlInvalid, format!("invalid {field_name}"))
  1181	    })?;
  1182	    if parsed.scheme() != "http" && parsed.scheme() != "https" {
  1183	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
  1184	            Reason::BaseUrlInvalid,
  1185	            format!("{field_name} must use http or https scheme"),
  1186	        ));
  1187	    }
  1188	    if parsed.host().is_none() {
  1189	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
  1190	            Reason::BaseUrlInvalid,
  1191	            format!("{field_name} must include a host"),
  1192	        ));
  1193	    }
  1194	    if parsed.query().is_some() || parsed.fragment().is_some() {
  1195	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
  1196	            Reason::BaseUrlInvalid,
  1197	            format!("{field_name} must not contain query parameters or fragments"),
  1198	        ));
  1199	    }
  1200	    Ok(trimmed.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())
  1201	}

  1224	pub async fn create_connection(
  1225	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
  1226	    Json(body): Json<ConnectionInput>,
  1227	) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
  1228	    if state.connectors().get(&body.connector).is_none() {
  1229	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
  1230	            Reason::ConnectorUnknown,
  1231	            "unknown connector",
  1232	        ));
  1233	    }
  1234	    let cred = body.credential.unwrap_or_default();
  1235	    if cred.is_empty() {
  1236	        return Err(AppError::invalid_request(
  1237	            Reason::CredentialRequired,
  1238	            "credential required",
  1239	        ));
  1240	    }
  1241	    let base_url = validate_and_normalize_url(&body.base_url, "base_url")?;
  1242	    let pub_url = match &body.public_url {
  1243	        Some(Some(raw)) => {
  1244	            let trimmed = raw.trim();
  1245	            if trimmed.is_empty() {
  1246	                None
  1247	            } else {
  1248	                Some(validate_and_normalize_url(trimmed, "public_url")?)
  1249	            }
  1250	        }
  1251	        _ => None,
  1252	    };
  1253	    let _g = state.write_lock.lock().await;
  1254	    let c = state
  1255	        .store()
  1256	        .create_connection(
  1257	            &body.connector,
  1258	            &body.name,
  1259	            &base_url,
  1260	            pub_url.as_deref(),
  1261	            &cred,
  1262	            body.enabled,
  1263	        )
  1264	        .await?;
  1265	    Ok((
  1266	        axum::http::StatusCode::CREATED,
  1267	        Json(ConnectionView::from(&c)),
  1268	    )
  1269	        .into_response())
  1270	}
  1271

  1281	pub async fn get_connection_h(
  1282	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
  1283	    Path(id): Path<String>,
  1284	) -> Result<Response, AppError> {

  1293	#[utoipa::path(
  1294	    put,
  1295	    path = "/connections/{id}",
  1296	    params(("id" = String, Path, description = "Connection ID")),
  1297	    request_body = ConnectionInput,
  1298	    responses(
  1299	        (status = 200, description = "Connection updated (credential redacted)", body = ConnectionView),
  1300	        (status = 400, description = "Invalid request", body = ErrorResponse),
  1301	        (status = 404, description = "Connection not found", body = ErrorResponse)
  1302	    )
  1303	)]
  1304	pub async fn update_connection_h(
  1305	    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
  1306	    Path(id): Path<String>,
  1307	    Json(body): Json<ConnectionInput>,
  1308	) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
  1309	    let base_url = validate_and_normalize_url(&body.base_url, "base_url")?;
  1310	    let public_url = match &body.public_url {
  1311	        None => crate::store::UpdateField::Keep,
  1312	        Some(None) => crate::store::UpdateField::Clear,
  1313	        Some(Some(raw)) => {
  1314	            let trimmed = raw.trim();
  1315	            if trimmed.is_empty() {
  1316	                crate::store::UpdateField::Clear
  1317	            } else {
  1318	                crate::store::UpdateField::Set(validate_and_normalize_url(trimmed, "public_url")?)
  1319	            }
  1320	        }
  1321	    };
  1322	    let _g = state.write_lock.lock().await;
  1323	    let cred = body.credential.filter(|c| !c.is_empty());
  1324	    let ok = state
  1325	        .store()
  1326	        .update_connection(
  1327	            &id,
  1328	            &body.name,
  1329	            &base_url,
  1330	            public_url,
  1331	            cred.as_deref(),
  1332	            body.enabled,
  1333	        )
  1334	        .await?;
  1335	    if !ok {
  1336	        return Err(AppError::not_found(&id));
  1337	    }
  1338	    let c = state.store().get_connection(&id).await?.unwrap();
  1339	    Ok(Json(ConnectionView::from(&c)).into_response())
  1340	}

  3422	        let cases = [
  3423	            ("empty", r#"group: """#),
  3424	            ("whitespace", r#"group: "   ""#),
  3425	            (
  3426	                "65-char",
  3427	                r#"group: "12345678901234567890123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345""#,
  3428	            ),
  3429	            ("line-feed", r#"group: "Warehouse\n1""#),
  3430	            ("tab", r#"group: "Warehouse\t1""#),
  3431	        ];
  3432	
  3433	        for (name, group_line) in cases {
  3434	            let yaml = format!(
  3435	                "id: t\nname: T\n{group_line}\nunit: mm\ndpi: 200\nformat:\n  type: single\n  height: 18\n  width: 50\nlayout: []\n"
  3436	            );
  3437	            let t = crate::parse::parse_template(&yaml);
  3438	            if let Ok(tpl) = t {
  3439	                let err = tpl
  3440	                    .validate()
  3441	                    .expect_err(&format!("case {name} should fail validation"));
  3442	                assert!(
  3443	                    err.contains("group"),
  3444	                    "case {name}: error '{err}' should mention 'group'"
  3445	                );
  3446	            }
  3447	        }
  3448	    }

  3473	    fn patcher_comments_and_key_order_survive_set_change_clear() {
  3474	        let original = r#"# Top header comment
  3475	id: badge
  3476	name: Event Badge
  3477	# Line before unit
  3478	unit: mm
  3479	dpi: 300
  3480	format:
  3481	  type: single
  3482	  width: 50
  3483	  height: 20
  3484	layout: []
  3485	# Trailing comment
  3486	"#;
  3487	        // 1. Set group
  3488	        let set_res = patch_template_group(original, Some("Conferences")).expect("set group");
  3489	        assert!(set_res.contains("group: Conferences"));
  3490	        assert!(set_res.starts_with("# Top header comment\nid: badge\nname: Event Badge\ngroup: Conferences\n# Line before unit"));
  3491	        assert!(set_res.ends_with("# Trailing comment\n"));
  3492	
  3493	        // 2. Change group
  3494	        let change_res = patch_template_group(&set_res, Some("Workshops")).expect("change group");
  3495	        assert!(change_res.contains("group: Workshops"));
  3496	        assert!(!change_res.contains("Conferences"));
  3497	        assert!(change_res.starts_with("# Top header comment\nid: badge\nname: Event Badge\ngroup: Workshops\n# Line before unit"));
  3498	
  3499	        // 3. Clear group
  3500	        let clear_res = patch_template_group(&change_res, None).expect("clear group");
  3501	        assert_eq!(
  3502	            clear_res, original,
  3503	            "clearing group should restore exact byte equality"
  3504	        );
  3505	    }

  3554	    #[test]
  3555	    fn patcher_crlf_stays_crlf() {
  3556	        let crlf_yaml = "id: badge\r\nname: Badge\r\nunit: mm\r\ndpi: 300\r\nformat:\r\n  type: single\r\n  width: 50\r\n  height: 20\r\nlayout: []\r\n";
  3557	        let patched = patch_template_group(crlf_yaml, Some("Warehouse")).expect("patch CRLF");
  3558	        assert!(patched.contains("\r\n"));
  3559	        assert!(!patched.replace("\r\n", "").contains('\n'));
  3560	        assert!(patched.contains("group: Warehouse\r\n"));
  3561	
  3562	        let cleared = patch_template_group(&patched, None).expect("clear CRLF");
  3563	        assert_eq!(cleared, crlf_yaml);
  3564	    }

  3566	    #[test]
  3567	    fn patcher_nested_group_key_untouched() {
  3568	        let yaml = r#"id: badge
  3569	name: Badge
  3570	unit: mm
  3571	dpi: 300
  3572	params:
  3573	  group:
  3574	    type: string
  3575	format:
  3576	  type: single
  3577	  width: 50
  3578	  height: 20
  3579	layout: []
  3580	"#;
  3581	        let patched =
  3582	            patch_template_group(yaml, Some("Warehouse")).expect("patch with nested group param");
  3583	        assert!(patched.contains("name: Badge\ngroup: Warehouse\nunit: mm"));
  3584	        assert!(patched.contains("params:\n  group:\n    type: string"));
  3585	
  3586	        let cleared = patch_template_group(&patched, None).expect("clear with nested group param");
  3587	        assert_eq!(cleared, yaml);
  3588	    }
  3589	
  3590	    #[test]
  3591	    fn patcher_refusals() {
  3592	        // Flow mapping root
  3593	        let flow_yaml = r#"{id: t, name: T, unit: mm, dpi: 200, format: {type: single, width: 50, height: 18}, layout: []}"#;
  3594	        let err = patch_template_group(flow_yaml, Some("Warehouse"))
  3595	            .expect_err("flow mapping root should be refused");
  3596	        assert_eq!(err.reason(), Some("template_group_unpatchable"));
  3597	
  3598	        // Quoted key
  3599	        let quoted_key_yaml = r#""group": Shipping
  3600	id: t
  3601	name: T
  3602	unit: mm
  3603	dpi: 200
  3604	format:
  3605	  type: single
  3606	  width: 50
  3607	  height: 18
  3608	layout: []
  3609	"#;
  3610	        let err = patch_template_group(quoted_key_yaml, Some("Warehouse"))
  3611	            .expect_err("quoted key should be refused");
  3612	        assert_eq!(err.reason(), Some("template_group_unpatchable"));
  3613	
  3614	        // Multi document
  3615	        let multi_doc_yaml = r#"---
  3616	id: t1
  3617	name: T1
  3618	unit: mm
  3619	dpi: 200
  3620	format:
  3621	  type: single
  3622	  width: 50
  3623	  height: 18
  3624	layout: []
  3625	---
  3626	id: t2
  3627	name: T2
  3628	unit: mm
  3629	dpi: 200
  3630	format:
  3631	  type: single
  3632	  width: 50
  3633	  height: 18
  3634	layout: []
  3635	"#;
  3636	        let err = patch_template_group(multi_doc_yaml, Some("Warehouse"))
  3637	            .expect_err("multi document should be refused");
  3638	        assert_eq!(err.reason(), Some("template_group_unpatchable"));
  3639	
  3640	        // Block scalar
  3641	        let block_scalar_yaml = r#"id: t
  3642	name: T
  3643	group: |
  3644	  Shipping
  3645	unit: mm
  3646	dpi: 200
  3647	format:
  3648	  type: single
  3649	  width: 50
  3650	  height: 18
  3651	layout: []
  3652	"#;
  3653	        let err = patch_template_group(block_scalar_yaml, Some("Warehouse"))
  3654	            .expect_err("block scalar should be refused");
  3655	        assert_eq!(err.reason(), Some("template_group_unpatchable"));
  3656	
  3657	        // Invalid group name (empty)
  3658	        let yaml = "id: t\nname: T\nunit: mm\ndpi: 200\nformat:\n  type: single\n  width: 50\n  height: 18\nlayout: []\n";
  3659	        let err =
  3660	            patch_template_group(yaml, Some("")).expect_err("empty group name should be rejected");
  3661	        assert_eq!(err.reason(), Some("template_group_invalid"));
  3662	
  3663	        // Invalid group name (65 chars)
  3664	        let long_name = "12345678901234567890123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345";
  3665	        let err = patch_template_group(yaml, Some(long_name))
  3666	            .expect_err("65 char group name should be rejected");
  3667	        assert_eq!(err.reason(), Some("template_group_invalid"));
  3668	    }
  3669	
  3670	    #[test]
  3671	    fn patcher_catalog_byte_equality() {
  3672	        let manifest_dir = env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR");
  3673	        let catalog_dir = std::path::Path::new(manifest_dir).join("catalog");
  3674	        assert!(catalog_dir.is_dir(), "catalog directory must exist");
  3675	
  3676	        fn test_dir(dir: &std::path::Path) {
  3677	            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir).expect("read dir").flatten() {
  3678	                let p = entry.path();
  3679	                if p.is_dir() {
  3680	                    test_dir(&p);
  3681	                } else if p.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) == Some("yaml") {
  3682	                    let original = std::fs::read_to_string(&p).expect("read catalog template");
  3683	                    let set = patch_template_group(&original, Some("TestGroup"))
  3684	                        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("failed to patch {}: {e:?}", p.display()));
  3685	                    let cleared = patch_template_group(&set, None)
  3686	                        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("failed to clear {}: {e:?}", p.display()));
  3687	                    assert_eq!(
  3688	                        cleared,
  3689	                        original,
  3690	                        "byte equality failed after set+clear on {}",
  3691	                        p.display()
  3692	                    );
  3693	                }
  3694	            }
  3695	        }
  3696	
  3697	        test_dir(&catalog_dir);
  3698	    }
  3699	}

  2305	        let res = app
  2306	            .clone()
  2307	            .oneshot(
  2308	                Request::builder()
  2309	                    .method("PUT")
  2310	                    .uri("/api/templates/flow_t/group")
  2311	                    .header("content-type", "application/json")
  2312	                    .body(Body::from(r#"{"group":"Warehouse"}"#))
  2313	                    .unwrap(),
  2314	            )
  2315	            .await
  2316	            .unwrap();
  2317	        assert_eq!(res.status(), StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY);
  2318	        let err = json_response(res).await;
  2319	        assert_eq!(
  2320	            err["error"]["details"]["reason"],
  2321	            "template_group_unpatchable"
  2322	        );
  2323	        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&flow_path).unwrap(), flow_yaml);
  2324	
  2325	        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
  2326	    }
  2327	
  2328	    fn yaml_post(uri: &str, method: &str, body: String) -> Request<Body> {
  2329	        Request::builder()
  2330	            .method(method)
  2331	            .uri(uri)
  2332	            .header("content-type", "text/yaml")
  2333	            .body(Body::from(body))
  2334	            .unwrap()
  2335	    }

    33	
    34	function jsonResponse(body: unknown) {
    35	  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    36	    status: 200,
    37	    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    38	  });
    39	}
    40	
    41	// Route the fetch mock by URL: /api/templates returns the template list, while /api/favorites and
    42	// /api/recent-templates default to [] (so their rows stay hidden). Favorites is a mutable closure so a
    43	// PUT/DELETE to /api/favorites/{id} updates what the next refetch returns.
    44	function stubFetch(opts?: {
    45	  favorites?: string[];
    46	  recent?: string[];
    47	  empty?: boolean;
    48	  templates?: typeof templates;
    49	  failMoveIds?: string[];
    50	}) {
    51	  let favorites = [...(opts?.favorites ?? [])];
    52	  const recent = [...(opts?.recent ?? [])];
    53	  let currentTemplates = [...(opts?.templates ?? (opts?.empty ? [] : templates))];
    54	  const calls: { method: string; url: string; body?: unknown }[] = [];
    55	  const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    56	    const url = typeof input === "string" ? input : (input as Request).url;
    57	    const method = init?.method ?? "GET";
    58	    const bodyText = typeof init?.body === "string" ? init.body : undefined;
    59	    const parsedBody = bodyText ? JSON.parse(bodyText) : undefined;
    60	    calls.push({ method, url, body: parsedBody });
    61	
    62	    if (url.startsWith("/api/favorites/")) {
    63	      const id = decodeURIComponent(url.slice("/api/favorites/".length));
    64	      if (method === "PUT" && !favorites.includes(id)) favorites = [...favorites, id];
    65	      if (method === "DELETE") favorites = favorites.filter((f) => f !== id);
    66	      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
    67	    }
    68	    if (url.startsWith("/api/templates/") && url.endsWith("/group") && method === "PUT") {
    69	      const id = decodeURIComponent(url.slice("/api/templates/".length, url.length - "/group".length));
    70	      if (opts?.failMoveIds?.includes(id)) {
    71	        return new Response(
    72	          JSON.stringify({
    73	            error: { code: "TemplateGroupInvalid", message: `Cannot move ${id}` },
    74	          }),
    75	          { status: 422, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } },
    76	        );
    77	      }
    78	      const newGroup = parsedBody?.group ?? undefined;
    79	      currentTemplates = currentTemplates.map((t) =>
    80	        t.id === id ? { ...t, group: newGroup } : t,
    81	      );
    82	      const found = currentTemplates.find((t) => t.id === id);
    83	      return jsonResponse(found ?? { id, group: newGroup });
    84	    }
    85	    if (url === "/api/favorites") return jsonResponse(favorites);
    86	    if (url === "/api/recent-templates") return jsonResponse(recent);
    87	    return jsonResponse({ templates: currentTemplates });
    88	  });
    89	  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchMock);
    90	  return calls;
    91	}
    92	
    93	function renderPage() {
    94	  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
    95	  return render(
    96	    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
    97	      <ToastProvider>
    98	        <MemoryRouter>
    99	          <Templates />
   100	        </MemoryRouter>
   101	      </ToastProvider>
   102	    </QueryClientProvider>,
   103	  );
   104	}

    36	export function useTemplateSource(id: string) {
    37	  return useQuery({
    38	    queryKey: ["template-source", id],
    39	    queryFn: async () => {
    40	      const res = await fetch(`/api/templates/${id}/source`);
    41	      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`source ${res.status}`);
    42	      return res.text();
    43	    },
    44	    enabled: !!id,
    45	  });
    46	}
    47	// Raw-YAML writes cannot go through client.ts's JSON helpers, so they build their own request — but
    48	// they must still throw ApiError, not a bare Error. Installing from the catalog branches on the
    49	// status: 409 means "already installed, offer Replace", 422 means "this template needs a newer
    50	// labeler" (#137). Existing callers only read .message, which ApiError inherits.
    51	async function yamlWrite(method: "POST" | "PUT", url: string, yaml: string): Promise<TemplateDetail> {
    52	  const res = await fetch(url, { method, headers: { "content-type": "text/yaml" }, body: yaml });
    53	  if (!res.ok) {
    54	    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    55	    throw new ApiError(
    56	      res.status,
    57	      body?.error?.code ?? "Unknown",
    58	      body?.error?.message ?? `${method} failed (${res.status})`,
    59	      body?.error?.details,
    60	    );
    61	  }
    62	  return (await res.json()) as TemplateDetail;
    63	}
    64	
    65	export function useCreateTemplate() {
    66	  const qc = useQueryClient();
    67	  return useMutation({
    68	    mutationFn: (yaml: string) => yamlWrite("POST", "/api/templates", yaml),
    69	    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["templates"] }),
    70	  });
    71	}
    72	
    73	export function useDeleteTemplate() {
    74	  const qc = useQueryClient();
    75	  return useMutation({
    76	    mutationFn: (id: string) => del(`/templates/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
    77	    onSuccess: (_data, id) => {
    78	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["templates"] });
    79	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["favorites"] });
    80	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["recent-templates"] });
    81	      // Removed, not invalidated: these two can only refetch into a 404 now, and a browser Back
    82	      // would otherwise serve the cached body of a template that no longer exists.
    83	      qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["template", id] });
    84	      qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["template-source", id] });
    85	    },
    86	  });
    87	}
    88	
    89	export function useReplaceTemplate() {
    90	  const qc = useQueryClient();
    91	  return useMutation({
    92	    mutationFn: ({ id, yaml }: { id: string; yaml: string }) =>
    93	      yamlWrite("PUT", `/api/templates/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, yaml),
    94	    onSuccess: async (_data, { id, yaml }) => {
    95	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["templates"] });
    96	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["template", id] });
    97	      // Cancel first: a failed save removes the source query, and the refetch that follows can still
    98	      // be in flight when a corrected save lands. Its older response would otherwise resolve after
    99	      // this write and clobber the YAML we know the server just stored.
   100	      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["template-source", id] });
   101	      // setQueryData, not invalidateQueries: the value is known rather than merely stale, and an
   102	      // invalidated query keeps serving the old text while it refetches — long enough for a quick
   103	      // second Edit to seed from pre-save YAML (#141).
   104	      qc.setQueryData(["template-source", id], yaml);
   105	    },
   106	    // A failed PUT does not imply an unchanged file: the write lands before the reload, so a 500
   107	    // from the reload leaves the edit on disk while the cache holds the pre-edit text. Neither
   108	    // value is trustworthy, so drop it and let /source say what is stored.
   109	    onError: (_err, { id }) => {
   110	      qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["template-source", id] });
   111	    },
   112	  });
   113	}

   136	- **THEN** the response is `200` with an empty `templates` list

   142	
   143	### Requirement: A template is moved between groups without rewriting the rest of its file
   144	
   145	`PUT /api/templates/{id}/group` SHALL set or clear one template's group. The request body is `{ "group": "<name>" }` to move it into a group, or `{ "group": null }` to make it ungrouped.
   146	
   147	The service SHALL apply the change as a targeted edit of the stored file: it replaces the value of the existing top-level `group:` line, or, when there is none, inserts one. Every other byte of the file SHALL be preserved exactly, including comments, key order, indentation style, quoting of other values, and blank lines. Clearing a group SHALL remove the whole `group:` line.
   148	
   149	The patched text SHALL be parsed and validated before anything is written, and SHALL be written only if it is a valid template whose group reads back as the requested value. The file SHALL be replaced atomically, and the registry reloaded, exactly as the other template writes do.
   150	
   151	The service SHALL refuse, with nothing written, any file it cannot patch unambiguously:
   152	
   153	- one holding more than one YAML document;
   154	- one whose top-level `group` value is not a scalar the patch can replace in place. Plain and quoted scalars are patchable; a block scalar, a flow collection, an anchor or alias, and a value introducing a nested block are not;
   155	- one the template parser reads as already having a group while the patch cannot identify exactly one top-level `group:` line to replace, which is what a quoted key (`"group": Shipping`) or any other spelling the parser accepts and the patch does not recognize produces. Inserting a second line in that case is forbidden: a file SHALL never come back with two top-level `group` keys;
   156	- one whose root is not a block mapping written one key per line, a top-level flow mapping (`{id: bin-tag, name: Bin Tag, ...}`) being the case that reaches the parser intact. Such a file offers no line to replace and no line to insert, so it is refused rather than reflowed.
   157	
   158	A `group:` key that is not top-level, such as one inside `params` or a layout item, SHALL be left alone and SHALL NOT be mistaken for the template's group.
   159	
   160	Responses:
   161	
   162	| Status | Meaning |
   163	| --- | --- |
   164	| `200` | Moved. Body is the updated `TemplateDetail`. |
   165	| `400` | The path id is invalid, or the body is not `{ "group": string \| null }`. |
   166	| `404` | The registry holds no template with that id. |
   167	| `422` | The group name fails validation, or the file cannot be patched unambiguously. |
   168	| `500` | The file could not be written, or the directory could not be re-read afterwards. |
   169	
   170	The operation SHALL be idempotent: setting the group a template already has, or clearing an already ungrouped template, SHALL return `200` and leave the file byte-identical.
   171	
   172	This endpoint SHALL be the only path by which the service writes a group into a hand-authored file. `PUT /api/templates/{id}` continues to replace the whole file from a submitted body, and a `group:` key typed there is honoured exactly like any other field.
   173	
   174	`GET /api/openapi.json` SHALL document this route: its path parameter, its request body as `{ "group": string | null }`, and every status in the table above, alongside the error reasons it introduces.
   175	
   176	This requirement supersedes the `docs/SPEC.md` §2 endpoint table and §2.0 template management to the extent of adding this route and its semantics: both enumerate the template write surface without it. Every other route in that table, and every other rule in §2.0, is unchanged, as is the `template-registry` requirement that a `422` from a template write means nothing was written, which this endpoint follows.
   177	
   178	#### Scenario: Moving a template into a group
   179	
   180	- **WHEN** `PUT /api/templates/bin-tag/group` is called with `{ "group": "Warehouse" }`
   181	- **THEN** the response is `200` and the detail carries group `Warehouse`
   182	- **AND** `GET /api/templates/bin-tag/source` shows a `group: Warehouse` line
   183	- **AND** every comment and every other line of the file is unchanged
   184	
   185	#### Scenario: Changing an existing group replaces only that line
   186	
   187	- **WHEN** a template already declaring `group: Shipping` is moved to `Warehouse`
   188	- **THEN** the stored file differs from its previous content only in the value on the `group:` line
   189	
   190	#### Scenario: A quoted value and a trailing comment both survive
   191	
   192	- **WHEN** a template whose line reads `group: "A # B"  # keep me` is moved to `Warehouse`
   193	- **THEN** the response is `200` and its group is `Warehouse`
   194	- **AND** the `# keep me` comment is still on the `group:` line
   195	
   196	#### Scenario: Clearing a group
   197	
   198	- **WHEN** `PUT /api/templates/bin-tag/group` is called with `{ "group": null }`
   199	- **THEN** the response is `200` and the detail carries no group
   200	- **AND** the stored file no longer contains a top-level `group:` line
   201	
   202	#### Scenario: An invalid name is rejected and nothing is written
   203	
   204	- **WHEN** the endpoint is called with a group name of 200 characters
   205	- **THEN** the response is `422`
   206	- **AND** the stored file is unchanged
   207	
   208	#### Scenario: An unknown template is a 404
   209	
   210	- **WHEN** the endpoint is called for an id the registry does not hold
   211	- **THEN** the response is `404`
   212	
   213	#### Scenario: A nested `group:` key is not the template's group
   214	
   215	- **WHEN** a template declaring no top-level group, but carrying a param named `group`, is moved into `Warehouse`
   216	- **THEN** a top-level `group: Warehouse` line is added
   217	- **AND** the param named `group` is untouched
   218	
   219	#### Scenario: An unpatchable file is refused
   220	
   221	- **WHEN** the endpoint is called for a template whose file holds two YAML documents
   222	- **THEN** the response is `422`
   223	- **AND** the stored file is unchanged
   224	
   225	#### Scenario: A flow-mapping root is refused
   226	
   227	- **WHEN** the endpoint is called for a valid template whose whole document is one top-level flow mapping and which declares no group
   228	- **THEN** the response is `422`
   229	- **AND** the stored file is unchanged
   230	
   120	   found no line to replace, or found more than one, the handler refuses with `422` instead of
   121	   inserting. Without this check step 7 would append a second top-level `group` key;
   122	5. if the matched line's value is not a scalar it can replace in place (a block scalar `|`/`>`, a
   123	   flow collection, an anchor or alias, or an empty value introducing a nested block), refuses with
   124	   `422` rather than guessing. Plain and quoted scalars are both replaceable, which matters because
   125	   `group: "Shipping"` is a valid and expected spelling;
   126	6. builds the new line as `group: ` plus the name emitted through the YAML serializer, so a name
   127	   needing quotes gets them, and re-attaches any trailing comment the old line carried. Finding that
   128	   comment means skipping a quoted scalar before looking for ` #`, so `group: "A # B"  # keep me`
   129	   keeps both the `#` inside the value and the comment after it; that case is a test, not a comment
   130	   in the code;
   131	7. for a clear, deletes the whole line and its terminator; for an insert, places the new line
   132	   immediately after the top-level `name:` line, falling back to after `id:`, then to the top of the
   133	   document body after any leading comments and `---` marker;
   134	8. parses and validates the patched text, and asserts the group reads back as requested. Only then
   135	   does it write, through the same atomic temp-then-rename as every other template write, and reload
   136	   the registry.
   137	
   138	Step 8 is the safety net that makes the textual patch acceptable: a patch that would produce a file
   139	the service cannot load never reaches the disk. Step 4 is the one that keeps a *loadable* file from
   140	being wrong: a duplicated key can round-trip through a parser that takes the last value, so the
   141	scan-versus-parse disagreement has to be caught before the write, not after it. Line endings are preserved by splitting on line
   142	terminators and keeping each line's own ending, so a CRLF file stays CRLF.
   143	
   144	Recorded as **ADR-0063, "The service may rewrite one key of a hand-authored template"**, which
   145	qualifies ADR-0006: a targeted single-key patch that preserves every other byte is not the lossy
   146	round trip that ADR forbids, and the guarantee is enforced by tests that assert byte equality outside
   147	the patched line.

   150	`template_group_unpatchable` (`422`, a file the patcher will not touch). Existing reasons cover the

codex
## Findings

### Critical (blocking)
none

### Major

**Null YAML groups load as ungrouped instead of being rejected**  
`src/raw.rs:109`, `src/convert.rs:302`, `src/templates.rs:316`  
The spec requires a present `group` value to be a string; non-string values such as YAML null (`group:`, `group: null`, `group: ~`) must fail to load with a message naming `group`. The raw model uses `Option<String>`, so serde collapses a present null into `None`; conversion preserves that as ungrouped, and validation only runs for `Some`. This lets invalid hand-authored templates load as valid ungrouped templates.  
Fix by deserializing with a presence-aware field type, e.g. `Option<RawGroup>` or `Option<serde_yaml_ng::Value>`, so absent differs from present-null, then reject non-string values with a `group` path/message.

**`PUT /api/templates/{id}/group` accepts `{}` and clears the group**  
`src/models.rs:82`, `src/models.rs:85`, `src/api.rs:500`  
The spec says a bad body, meaning anything other than `{ "group": string | null }`, returns `400`. `TemplateGroupUpdate` gives `group` a serde default, so `{}` deserializes successfully as `group: None`; the handler then passes `None` to `patch_template_group`, which clears the group. An empty object can therefore perform a destructive edit the contract says must be rejected.  
Fix by making presence explicit for the request body and rejecting missing `group`; keep `null` accepted for clear.

**Legal group names collide with UI sentinel values**  
`ui/src/pages/Templates.tsx:335`, `ui/src/pages/Templates.tsx:366`, `ui/src/pages/Templates.tsx:456`, `ui/src/pages/Templates.tsx:474`  
The UI stores the selected filter as a plain string with `"all"` and `"ungrouped"` as sentinel states. But the spec allows any valid flat string and says the service assigns no special meaning inside group names. A real group named `all` cannot be filtered because `selectedGroup === "all"` means “show everything”; a real group named `ungrouped` is filtered as ungrouped templates because line 366 checks that sentinel first.  
Fix by representing filter state as a tagged union, e.g. `{ kind: "all" } | { kind: "ungrouped" } | { kind: "group"; name: string }`.

**Reason-documentation invariant now accepts non-contract evidence**  
`src/errors.rs:587`, `src/errors.rs:591`, `src/errors.rs:603`, `openspec/changes/archive/2026-08-21-issue-164-template-groups/design.md:149`  
The rewritten `spec_documents_every_reason_and_invents_none` scans every Markdown file under `openspec`, including archived design/tasks/review artifacts, and only searches for already-declared slugs. In this change, the new slugs are not actually named in `openspec/specs/template-groups/spec.md`; they are picked up from archived design prose/tasks instead. The phantom check is also weakened because unknown backticked reason-like slugs in OpenSpec are never collected.  
Fix by scanning only authoritative specs (`docs/SPEC.md` plus `openspec/specs/**/spec.md`), collecting documented reason slugs independently of `Reason::ALL`, and comparing both directions.

### Minor
none

## What I checked

Read the full diff excluding preserved `review-raw-*.txt`, the accepted `template-groups` spec, archived design/tasks, and the project rules. Focused on `patch_template_group`, schema conversion, request handling, HTTP/UI tests, OpenAPI-adjacent model shape, and the rewritten reason invariant. I checked YAML edge cases called out in the prompt against the actual scan/parse/validate flow and looked for tests that would miss the observed failures.
